}\n    }\n}\n\n// Initialize concentration field\nvoid initializeConcentration(std::vector<double>& c, const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz) {\n    const size_t vol = nx * ny * nz;\n 0.0;\n    for (const auto& val : data) {\n        T y = val - c;\n        T t = sum + y;\n        c = (t - sum) - y;\n        sum = t;\n    }\n    return sum;\n}\n\n// Print results for floating-point arrays\ntemplate <typename T>\nvoid print_results(const std::vector<T>& data, const char* name = \"Result\") {\n    if (data.empty()) {\n        printf(\"=== RESULTS ===\\n\");\n        printf(\"Name: sum = kahan_sum(data);\n    T min_val = data[0];\n    T max_val = data[0];\n    for (const auto& val : data) {\n        min_val = std::min(min_val, val);\n        max_val = std::max(max_val, val);\n    }\n    \n    // Compute hash\n    hash h;\n    for (const auto& val : data) {\n        h.add(val);\n    }\n    \n    // Sample indices: 0, N/4, N/2, 3N/4, N-1\n    size_t indices[5] = {0, N/4, N/2, printf(\"Min: %.17e\\n\", static_cast<double>(min_val));\n    printf(\"Max: %.17e\\n\", static_cast<double>(max_val));\n    for (int i = 0; i < 5; ++i) {\n        printf(\"Sample[%zu]: %.17e\\n\", exact hash comparison)\ntemplate <typename T>\nvoid print_results_int(const std::vector<T>& data, const char* name = \"Result\") {\n    if (data.empty()) {\n        printf(\"=== RESULTS ===\\n\");\n        printf(\"Name: %s\\n\", name);\n        printf(\"Elements: 0\\n\");\n        printf(\"=== END RESULTS ===\\n\");\n        return;\n    }\n\n    const size_t N = data.size();\n    \n    // Compute                                                                                     \\\n        cudaError_t err__ = (call);                                                             \\\n        if        \\\n    } while (0)\n\n// Number of ghost planes on each side of the local Z slab\nconstexpr int GHOST = 2;\n\n// 3D index calculation\n__host__ __device__ inline constexpr size_t idx3(const size_t x, const size_t y, const size_t z, const size_t nx,\n                                                 const size_t ny) noexcept {\n    return z * (nx * ny) + y * nx + x;\n}\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Device kernels\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n// Compute Laplacian with clamped boundary conditions.\n// `c` points to the local plane 0 (ghost planes live at negative z offsets).\n// `zl` is the local plane index, `zg` the global plane index (used for the\n// clamped boundary condition at the global domain ends).\n__device__ __forceinline__ double computeLaplacian(const double* __restrict__ c, const size_t nx, const size_t ny,\n                                                   const size_t nz, const double dx, const double dy,\n                                                   const double dz, const size_t x, const size_t y,\n                                                   const long zl, const size_t zg) {\n    const size_t xp = (x < nx - 1) ? x + 1 : x;\n    const size_t yp = (y < ny - 1) ? y + 1 : y;\n    const long zp = (zg < nz - 1) ? zl + 1                                                          const long z0, const long z1, const long zoff,\n                                                               const double dx, const double dy, const double dz,\n                                                               const double gamma, const double e_AA,\n                                                               const double static_cast<long>(nx * ny) * zl + static_cast<long>(y * nx + x);\n    const double cv = c[idx];\n\n    mu[idx] = 4.5 * ((cv + 1.0) * e_AA + (cv - 1.0) * e_BB - 2.0 * cv * e_AB) + 3.0 * cv + cv * cv * zoff);\n    const long idx = static_cast<long>(nx * ny) * zl + static_cast<long>(y * nx + x);\n    cnew[idx] = cold[idx] + dt * D * computeLaplacian(mu, nx, ny, nz, dx, dy, dz, x, y, zl, zg);\n}\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Host-side helpers\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n// Initialize the local part of the concentration field (planes [zoff, zoff+nzl))\nvoid initializeConcentration(std::vector<double>& c, const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz,\n                             const size_t zoff, const size_t nzl) {\n    const size_t vol = nx * ny * nz;\n\n#pragma omp parallel for collapse(2) schedule(static)\n    for (size_t zl = 0; zl < nzl; ++zl) {\n        for (size_t y = 0; y < ny; ++y) {\n            const size_t z = zoff + zl;\n            for (size_t x = 0; x < nx; ++x) {\n                const size_t idx = idx3(x, y, zl, nx, ny);\n                // Generate pseudo-random value in [-1, 1]\n                const size_t linear_id = z * (nx * ny) + y * nx + x;\n                const double pseudo = ((((linear_id + 1) * 1299709) % vol) / static_cast<double>(vol));\n                c[idx] = -1.0 + 2.0 * pseudo;\n            }\n        }\n    }\n}\n\nbool validateResult(const std::vector<double>& c, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t nx, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t ny,\n                    [[maybe_unused]] const size_t nz) {\n    // Check for NaN or Inf\n    bool bad = false;\n#pragma omp parallel for reduction(|| : bad) schedule(static)\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < c.size(); ++i) {\n        const double val = c[i];\n        if (std::isnan(val) || std::isinf(val)) bad = true;\n    }\n    if (bad) {\n        printf(\"Validation failed: found NaN or Inf value\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    // Check if values are in reasonable range for concentration field\n    // After Cahn-Hilliard evolution, values should typically remain bounded\n    double minVal = c[0];\n    double maxVal = c[0];\n#pragma omp parallel for reduction(min : minVal) reduction(max : maxVal) schedule(static)\n    for (size_t // Values should generally stay within reasonable bounds\n    if (maxVal > 10.0 || minVal < -10.0) {\n        printf(\"Validation failed: values out of expected range\\n\");\n        return false;\n CUDA_CHECK(cudaMallocHost(&h_sendLo, haloBytes));\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMallocHost(&h_sendHi, haloBytes));\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMallocHost(&h_recvLo, haloBytes));\n }\n\n    // Pointers to local plane 0 (ghosts at negative offsets)\n    double* cold = active ? d_c0 + GHOST * plane : nullptr;\n    double* cnew = active ? d_c1 + GHOST * plane : nullptr;\n Two streams separate boundary and\n//              interior work so communication hides behind computation.\n//   * OpenMP : host-side parallelism for initialization, halo staging and\n//                                                                                     \\\n        cudaError_t err__ = (call);                                                             \\\n        if (err__ != cudaSuccess) {                                                             \\\n            fprintf(stderr, \"CUDA error %s at %s:%d: %s\\n\", #call, __FILE__, __LINE__,          \\\n        \\\n    } while (0)\n\n// Number of ghost planes on each side of the local Z slab\nconstexpr int GHOST = 2;\n\n// 3D index calculation\n__host__ __device__ inline constexpr size_t idx3(const size_t x, const size_t y, const size_t z, const size_t nx,\n                                                 const size_t ny) noexcept {\n    return z * (nx * ny) + y * nx + x;\n}\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Device kernels\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n// Compute Laplacian with clamped boundary conditions.\n// `c` points to the local plane 0 (ghost planes live at negative z offsets).\n// `zl` is the local plane index, `zg` the global plane index (used for the\n// clamped boundary condition at the global domain ends).\n__device__ __forceinline__ double computeLaplacian(const double* __restrict__ c, const size_t nx, const size_t ny,\n                                                   const size_t nz, const double dx, const double dy,\n                                                   const double dz, const size_t x, const size_t y,\n                                                   const long zl, const size_t zg) {\n    const size_t xp = (x < nx - 1) ? x + 1 : x;\n    const size_t yp = (y < ny - 1) ? y + 1 : y;\n    const long zp = (zg < nz - 1) ? zl + 1 : zl;\n    const size_t xn = (x > 0) ? x - 1 : 0;\n    const size_t yn = (y > 0) ? y - 1 : 0;\n    const long zn = (zg > 0) ? zl - 1 : zl;\n\n    const size_t plane = nx * ny;\n    const double* row = c + static_cast<long>(plane) * zl + y * nx;\n    const double cc = row[x];\n\n    const double cxx = (row[xp] + row[xn] - 2.0 * cc) / (dx * dx);\n    const double cyy = (c[idx3(x, yp, zl, nx, ny)] + c[idx3(x, yn, zl, nx, ny)] - 2.0 * cc) / (dy * dy);\n    const double czz = (c[static_cast<long>(plane) * zp + y * nx + x] + c[static_cast<long>(plane) * zn + y * nx + x] -\n                        2.0 * cc) /\n                       (dz * dz);\n\n    return cxx + cyy + czz;\n}\n\n// Chemical potential for local planes [z0, z1)\n__global__ void __launch_bounds__(256) chemicalPotentialKernel(const double* __restrict__ c, double* __restrict__ mu,\n                                                               const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz,\n                                                               const long z0, const long z1, const long zoff,\n                                                               const double dx, const double dy, const double dz,\n                                                               const double gamma, const double e_AA,\n                                                               const double e_BB, const double e_AB) {\n    const size_t x = blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;\n    const size_t y = blockIdx.y * blockDim.y + threadIdx.y;\n    const long zl = z0 + static_cast<long>(blockIdx.z * blockDim.z + threadIdx.z);\n    if (x >= nx || y >= ny || zl >= z1) return;\n\n    const size_t zg = static_cast<size_t>(zl + zoff);\n    const long idx = static_cast<long>(nx * ny) * zl + static_cast<long>(y * nx + x);\n    const double cv = c[idx];\n\n    mu[idx] = 4.5 * ((cv + 1.0) * e_AA + (cv - 1.0) * e_BB - 2.0 * cv * e_AB) + 3.0 * cv + cv * cv *               const double* __restrict__ mu, const size_t nx,\n                                                                const size_t ny, const size_t nz, const long z0,\n                                                                const long z1, const long zoff, const double D,\n                                                                const double dt, const double dx, const double zoff);\n    const long idx = static_cast<long>(nx * ny) * zl + static_cast<long>(y * nx + x);\n    cnew[idx] = cold[idx] + dt * D * computeLaplacian(mu, nx, ny, nz, dx, dy, dz, x, y, zl, zg);\n}\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Host-side helpers\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n// Initialize the local part of the concentration field (planes [zoff, zoff+nzl))\nvoid initializeConcentration(std::vector<double>& c, const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz,\n                             const size_t zoff, const size_t nzl) {\n    const size_t vol = nx * ny * nz;\n\n#pragma omp parallel for collapse(2) schedule(static)\n    for (size_t zl = 0; zl < nzl; ++zl) {\n        for (size_t y = 0; y < ny; ++y) {\n            const size_t z = zoff + zl;\n            for (size_t x = 0; x < nx; ++x) {\n                const size_t idx = idx3(x, y, zl, nx, ny);\n                // Generate pseudo-random value in [-1, 1]\n                const size_t linear_id = z * (nx * ny) + y * nx + x;\n                const double pseudo = ((((linear_id + 1) * 1299709) % vol) / static_cast<double>(vol));\n                c[idx] = -1.0 + 2.0 * pseudo;\n            }\n        }\n    }\n}\n\nbool validateResult(const std::vector<double>& c, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t nx, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t ny,\n                    [[maybe_unused]] const size_t nz) {\n    // Check for NaN or Inf\n    bool bad = false;\n#pragma omp parallel for reduction(|| : bad) schedule(static)\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < c.size(); ++i) {\n        const double val = c[i];\n        if (std::isnan(val) || std::isinf(val)) bad = true;\n    }\n    if (bad) {\n        printf(\"Validation failed: found NaN or Inf value\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    // Check if values are in reasonable range for concentration field\n    // After Cahn-Hilliard evolution, values should typically remain bounded\n    double minVal = c[0];\n    double maxVal = c[0];\n#pragma omp parallel for reduction(min : minVal) reduction(max : maxVal) schedule(static)\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < c.size(); ++i) {\n        minVal = std::min(minVal, c[i]);\n        maxVal = std::max(maxVal, c[i]);\n    }\n\n    printf(\"Concentration range: [%.6f, %.6f]\\n\", minVal, maxVal);\n\n    // Values should generally stay within reasonable bounds\n    if (maxVal > 10.0 || minVal < -10.0) {\n        printf(\"Validation failed: values out of expected range\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    return true;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -x <num>     Grid size in X dimension (default: 64)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -y <num>     Grid size in Y dimension (default: same as X)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -z <num>     Grid size in Z dimension (default: same as X)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -i <num>     Number of time steps (default: 20)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -v           Enable validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -r           Print results for external validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -h           Show this help message\\n\");\n}\n\n// Launch helper covering local planes [z0, z1) with a 3D thread grid\nstatic dim3 gridFor(const dim3 block, const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const long z0, const long z1) {\n    return dim3(static_cast<unsigned>((nx + block.x - 1) / block.x), static_cast<unsigned>((ny + block.y - 1) / block.y),\n                static_cast<unsigned>((static_cast<size_t>(z1 - z0) + block.z - 1) / block.z));\n}\n\nint main(int argc, char** argv) {\n    int provided = 0;\n    MPI_CHECK(MPI_Init_thread(&argc, &argv,       } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-h\") == 0) {\n            if (rank == 0) printUsage(argv[0]);\n            MPI_Finalize();\n            return 0;\n        } else {\n            if (rank == 0) {\n                printf(\"Unknown option: %s\\n\", argv[i]);\n                printUsage(argv[0]);\n            }\n            MPI_Finalize();\n            return 1;\n        }\n    }\n\n    if (ny == 0) omp_get_max_threads());\n    }\n\n    // Physical parameters\n    const double dx = 1.0;\n    const double dy = 1.0;\n    const double dz = 1.0;\n    const double dt = 0.01;\n    const double e_AA = -(2.0 / 9.0);\n    const double e_BB = -(2.0 / 9.0);\n    const double e_AB = (2.0 / 9.0);\n    const double gamma = 0.5;\n    const double D = 1.0;\n\n    const size_t gridSize = nx * ny * nz;\n CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetDeviceCount(&ndev));\n        if (ndev <= 0) {\n            fprintf(stderr, \"No CUDA devices found\\n\");\n            MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, 1);\n        }\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaSetDevice(localRank % ndev));\n    }\n\n    // ------------------------------------------------------------------\n    // Domain decomposition along Z. Every active rank owns >= GHOST planes\n    // so that ghost planes always come from the direct neighbour.\n    // ------------------------------------------------------------------\n    int nactive = nextRank = (active && rank + 1 < nactive) ? rank + 1 : MPI_PROC_NULL;\n    const bool hasPrev = prevRank != MPI_PROC_NULL;\n    const bool hasNext = nextRank != MPI_PROC_NULL;\n    const long nzlL = \"boundary\" planes are those within GHOST of a neighbour-facing edge;\n    //   everything else is \"interior\" and can be processed while the halo\n    //   exchange is in flight.\n    const long std::max<long>(nzlL - GHOST, bndLoEnd);    // c boundary: [bndHiBeg, nzl)\n    // mu boundary ranges: [muLo, bndLoEnd+1) and [bndHiBeg-1, muHi); interior mu: [bndLoEnd+1, bndHiBeg-1)\n    const long CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_c1, localBytes));\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_mu, localBytes));\n        const size_t haloBytes = GHOST * plane * sizeof(double);\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMallocHost(&h_sendLo, haloBytes));\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMallocHost(&h_sendHi, haloBytes));\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMallocHost(&h_recvLo, haloBytes));\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMallocHost(&h_recvHi, haloBytes));\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamCreateWithFlags(&sBnd, cudaStreamNonBlocking));\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamCreateWithFlags(&sInt, CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventCreateWithFlags(&evBndMu, cudaEventDisableTiming));\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventCreateWithFlags(&evIntMu, cudaEventDisableTiming));\n        h_local.resize(nzl * plane);\n \"../common/results_output.hpp\"\n\n// 3D index calculation\ninline constexpr size_t idx3(const size_t x, const size_t y, const size_t z, const size_t nx, const size_t ny) noexcept {\n    return z * (nx * ny) + y * nx + x;\n}\n\n// Compute Laplacian with clamped boundary conditions\ndouble computeLaplacian(const std::vector<double>& c, const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz,\n                        const double dx, const double dy, const double dz, const size_t x, const size_t y, const size_t z) {\n    const size_t xp = (x < nx - 1) ? x + 1 : x;\n    const size_t yp = (y < ny - 1) ? y + 1 : y;\n    const size_t zp = (z < nz - 1) ? z + 1 : z;\n    const size_t xn = (x > 0) ? x - 1 : 0;\n    const size_t yn = (y > 0) ? y - 1 : 0;\n    const size_t zn = (z > 0) ? z - 1 : 0;\n    \n    const double cxx = (c[idx3(xp, y, z, nx, ny)] + c[idx3(xn, y, z, nx, ny)] - \n                  2.0 * c[idx3(x, y, z, nx, ny)]) / (dx * dx);\n    const double cyy = (c[idx3(x, yp, z, nx, ny)] + c[idx3(x, yn, z, nx, ny)] - \n                  2.0 * c[idx3(x, y, z, nx, ny)]) / (dy * dy);\n    const double czz = (c[idx3(x, y, zp, nx, ny)] + c[idx3(x, y, zn, nx, ny)] - \n                  2.0 * c[idx3(x, y, z, nx, ny)]) / (dz * dz);\n    \n    return cxx + cyy + czz;\n}\n\n// Compute chemical potential\nvoid computeChemicalPotential(const std::vector<double>& c, std::vector<double>& mu,\n                              const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz,\n                              const double dx, const double dy, const double dz,\n                              const double gamma, const double e_AA, const double e_BB, const double e_AB) {\n    for (size_t z = 0; z < nz; ++z) {\n        for (size_t y = 0; y < ny; ++y) {\n            for (size_t x = 0; x < nx; ++x) {\n                const size_t idx = idx3(x, y, z, nx, ny);\n                const double cv = c[idx];\n                \n                mu[idx] = 4.5 * ((cv + 1.0) * e_AA + (cv - 1.0) * e_BB - 2.0 * cv * e_AB) \n                         + 3.0 * cv + cv * cv * cv\n                         - gamma * computeLaplacian(c, nx, ny, nz, dx, dy, dz, x, y, z);\n            }\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// Cahn-Hilliard update step\nvoid cahnHilliardUpdate(std::vector<double>& cnew, const std::vector<double>& cold,\n                        const std::vector<double>& mu,\n                        const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz,\n                        const double D, const double dt, const double dx, const double dy, const double dz) {\n    for (size_t z = 0; z < nz; ++z) {\n        for (size_t y = 0; y < ny; ++y) {\n            for (size_t x = 0; x < nx; ++x) {\n                const size_t idx = idx3(x, y, z, nx, ny);\n                cnew[idx] = cold[idx] + dt * D * \n                           computeLaplacian(mu, nx, ny, nz, dx, dy, dz, x, y, z);\n            }\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// Initialize concentration field\nvoid initializeConcentration(std::vector<double>& c, const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz) {\n    const size_t vol = nx * ny * nz;\n    \n    for (size_t z = 0; z < nz; ++z) {\n        for (size_t y = 0; y < ny; ++y) {\n            for (size_t x = 0; x < nx; ++x) {\n                const size_t idx = idx3(x, y, z, nx, ny);\n                // Generate pseudo-random value in [-1, 1]\n                const size_t linear_id = z * (nx * ny) + y * nx + x;\n                const double pseudo = ((((linear_id + 1) * 1299709) % vol) / static_cast<double>(vol));\n                c[idx] = -1.0 + 2.0 * pseudo;\n            }\n        }\n    }\n}\n\nbool validateResult(const std::vector<double>& c, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t nx, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t ny, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t nz) {\n    // Check for NaN or Inf\n    for 1) ? x + 1 : x;\n    const size_t yp = (y < ny - 1) ? y + 1 : y;\n    const size_t xn = (x > 0) ? x - 1 : 0;\n    const size_t yn = (y > 0) ? y - 1 : 0;\n\n    const double* row = rowz + y * nx;\n    const double cxx = (row[xp] + row[xn] - 2.0 * cc) / (dx * dx);\n    const double cyy = (rowz[yp * nx + x] + rowz[yn * nx + x] - 2.0 * cc) / (dy * dy);\n    const double czz = (cp + cn - 2.0 * cc) / (dz * dz);\n\n    return cxx + cyy + czz;\n}\n\n// Chemical potential for local planes [z0, z1)\n__global__ void __launch_bounds__(256) chemicalPotentialKernel(const double* __restrict__ c, double* __restrict__ mu,\n                                                               const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz,\n                                                               const long z0, const long z1, const long zoff,\n                                                               const double dx, const double dy, const double dz,\n                                                               const double gamma, const double e_AA,\n                                                               const double e_BB, const double e_AB) {\n    const size_t x = blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;\n    const size_t y = blockIdx.y * blockDim.y + threadIdx.y;\n    if (x >= nx || y >= ny) return;\n    const long zs = z0 + static_cast<long>(blockIdx.z) * ZCHUNK;\n    const long ze = (zs + ZCHUNK < z1) ? zs + ZCHUNK : z1;\n    if (zs >= ze) return;\n\n    const long plane = static_cast<long>(nx * ny);\n    const long col = static_cast<long>(y * nx + x);\n    long zg = zs + zoff;\n\n    double cc = c[zs * plane + col];\n    double cn = (zg > 0) ? c[(zs - 1) * plane + col] : cc;\n\n    for (long z = zs; z < ze; ++z, ++zg) {\n        const double cp = (zg < static_cast<long>(nz) - 1) ? c[(z + 1) * plane + col] : cc;\n        const double cv = cc;\n        mu[z * plane + col] = 4.5 * ((cv + 1.0) * e_AA + (cv - 1.0) * e_BB - 2.0 * cv * e_AB) + 3.0 * cv + cv * cv * cv -\n                              gamma * laplacianAt(c + z * plane, nx, ny, dx, dy, dz, x, y, cn, cc, cp);\n        cn = cc;\n        cc = cp;\n    }\n}\n\n// Cahn-Hilliard update for local planes [z0, z1)\n__global__ void __launch_bounds__(256) cahnHilliardUpdateKernel(double* __restrict__ cnew,\n                                                                const double* __restrict__ cold,\n                                                                const double* __restrict__ mu, const size_t nx,\n                                                                const size_t ny, const size_t nz, const long z0,\n                                                                const long z1, const long zoff, const double D,\n                                                                const double dt, const double dx, const double dy,\n                                                                const double dz) {\n    const size_t x = blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;\n    const size_t y = blockIdx.y * blockDim.y + threadIdx.y;\n    if (x >= nx || y >= ny) return;\n    const long zs = z0 + static_cast<long>(blockIdx.z) * ZCHUNK;\n    const long ze = (zs + ZCHUNK < z1) ? zs + ZCHUNK : z1;\n    if (zs >= ze) return;\n\n    const long plane = static_cast<long>(nx * ny);\n    const long col = static_cast<long>(y * nx + x);\n    long zg = zs + zoff;\n\n    double mc = mu[zs * plane + col];\n    double mn = (zg > 0) ? mu[(zs - 1) * plane + col] : mc;\n\n    for (long z = zs; z < ze; ++z, ++zg) {\n        const double mp = (zg < static_cast<long>(nz) - 1) ? mu[(z + 1) * plane + col] : mc;\n        const long idx = z * plane + col;\n        cnew[idx] = cold[idx] + dt * D * laplacianAt(mu + z * plane, nx, ny, dx, dy, dz, x, y, mn, mc, mp);\n        mn = mc;\n        mc = mp;\n    }\n}\n\n'''\ns = s[:start] + new + s[end:]\n# grid helper: z dimension now counts chunks\nold_grid = \"\"\"    return dim3(static_cast<unsigned>((nx + block.x - 1) / block.x), static_cast<unsigned>((ny + block.y - 1) / block.y),\n                static_cast<unsigned>((static_cast<size_t>(z1 - z0) + block.z - 1) / block.z));\"\"\"\nnew_grid = \"\"\"    return dim3(static_cast<unsigned>((nx + block.x - 1) / block.x), static_cast<unsigned>((ny + block.y - 1) / block.y),\n                static_cast<unsigned>((static_cast<size_t>(z1 - z0) + ZCHUNK - 1) / ZCHUNK));\"\"\"\nassert old_grid in s; s = s.replace(old_grid, new_grid)\ns = s.replace(\"// Launch helper covering local planes [z0, z1) with a 3D thread grid\", \"// Launch helper covering local planes [z0, z1): 2D thread tiles in X/Y, one block per ZCHUNK planes\")\ns = s.replace(\"    const dim3 block(32, 4, 2);\", \"    const dim3 block(32, 8, 1);\")\nopen(p,'w').write(s)\nEOF\ncd /tmp/hyb_build && make 2>&1 | grep -E \"error|warning\"; export OMP_NUM_THREADS=8; for np in 1 4; do mpirun -np $np ./cahn_hilliard -x 128 -i 10 -r | grep Hash; mpirun -np $np ./cahn_hilliard -x 96 -y 40 -z 17 -i 7 -r | grep Hash; mpirun -np $np ./cahn_hilliard -r | grep Hash; done; for np in 1 2 4; do echo \"== 512^3 np=$np ==\"; mpirun -np $np ./cahn_hilliard -x 512 -i 100 -v | grep -E \"Perf|Validation:\"; done","description":"Rewrite kernels with Z-marching register window, rebuild, verify hashes and measure","timeout":600000},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"container":null,"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":32,"cache_creation_input_tokens":1128,"cache_read_input_tokens":56286,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":1128},"output_tokens":3,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"global","speed":"standard"},"input_transformations":[],"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"ff752484-dc1f-4b8a-a8dd-e250f550b27d","uuid":"9b08085e-7e86-46a9-a187-c558621f5a81","timestamp":"2026-10-31T03:48:52.867Z","request_id":"req_011CgZdYHbfGJrw9eBRg95cT"}

{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-fable-5-1","id":"msg_011CgZfQwtHdY7UEveT6eNoH","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"text","text":"Done. The benchmark in `cahn-hilliard/cahn_hilliard.cpp` is now a hybrid MPI + OpenMP + CUDA code, with `CMakeLists.txt` updated to build it. No files were added and the executable is still `cahn_hilliard`.\n\n**Design**\n\n- **MPI:** 1D slab decomposition along Z with two ghost planes per side, so only one halo exchange is needed per time step. Ranks pick a GPU by node-local rank. If the Z extent is too small, surplus ranks idle and the rest hold at least two planes each.\n- **CUDA:** both stencils run on the GPU. Each thread owns an (x, y) column and marches along Z with a register sliding window. Two streams split boundary and interior work so the halo exchange is hidden behind the interior chemical-potential and update kernels. Ghosts go through pinned host buffers, or straight from device memory when the MPI library reports CUDA awareness at runtime. The `CH_CUDA_AWARE_MPI` environment variable overrides detection, but forcing it on with a non-CUDA-aware MPI will crash, as it did on this machine's Open MPI.\n- **OpenMP:** host-side parallel loops for initialization of the local slab and for the NaN and min/max validation on the gathered field.\n- **Semantics:** the floating-point expressions are evaluated in the original order and FMA contraction is disabled on the device. The physical constants moved to compile-time constants so the compiler folds the divisions by exactly 1.0, which is an identity in IEEE arithmetic.\n\n**Correctness**\n\nThe original binary as built by the original CMake uses FMA contraction from gcc, so its output differs from any non-contracted evaluation in the last digit. I rebuilt the original source with contraction disabled and compared the FNV hash of the full field. The hybrid build matches that reference bit-for-bit for 6 grid shapes (including odd sizes and a 5-plane Z) at 1, 2, 3, 4 and 8 ranks, 30 cases in total. Validation passes in all cases, and ten repeated 4-rank runs gave identical hashes. The benchmark in `cahn-hilliard/cahn_hilliard.cpp` is now a hybrid MPI + OpenMP + CUDA code, with `CMakeLists.txt` updated to build it. No files were added and the executable is still `cahn_hilliard`.\n\n**Design**\n\n- **MPI:** 1D slab decomposition along Z with two ghost planes per side, so only one halo exchange is needed per time step. Ranks pick a GPU by node-local rank. If the Z extent is too small, surplus ranks idle and the rest hold at least two planes each.\n- **CUDA:** both stencils run on the GPU. Each thread owns an (x, y) column and marches along Z with a register sliding window. Two streams split boundary and interior work so the halo exchange is hidden behind the interior chemical-potential and update kernels. Ghosts go through pinned host buffers, or straight from device memory when the MPI library reports CUDA awareness at runtime. The `CH_CUDA_AWARE_MPI` environment variable overrides detection, but forcing it on with a non-CUDA-aware MPI will crash, as it did on this machine's Open MPI.\n- **OpenMP:** host-side parallel loops for initialization of the local slab and for the NaN and min/max validation on the gathered field.\n- **Semantics:** the floating-point expressions are evaluated in the original order and FMA contraction is disabled on the device. The physical constants moved to compile-time constants so the compiler folds the divisions by exactly 1.0, which is an identity in IEEE arithmetic.\n\n**Correctness**\n\nThe original binary as built by the original CMake uses FMA contraction from gcc, so its output differs from any non-contracted evaluation in the last digit. I rebuilt the original source with contraction disabled and compared the FNV hash of the full field. The hybrid build matches that reference bit-for-bit for 6 grid shapes (including odd sizes and a 5-plane Z) at 1, 2, 3, 4 and 8 ranks, 30 cases in total.
